	private void ItemCheck_GetMeleeHitbox(Item sItem, Rectangle heldItemFrame, out bool dontAttack, out Rectangle itemRectangle)
	{
		dontAttack = false;
		itemRectangle = new Rectangle((int)itemLocation.X, (int)itemLocation.Y, 32, 32);
		if (!Main.dedServ)
		{
			int num = heldItemFrame.Width;
			int num2 = heldItemFrame.Height;
			switch (sItem.type)
			{
			case 5094:
				num -= 10;
				num2 -= 10;
				break;
			case 5095:
				num -= 10;
				num2 -= 10;
				break;
			case 5096:
				num -= 12;
				num2 -= 12;
				break;
			case 5097:
				num -= 8;
				num2 -= 8;
				break;
			}
			itemRectangle = new Rectangle((int)itemLocation.X, (int)itemLocation.Y, num, num2);
		}
		float adjustedItemScale = GetAdjustedItemScale(sItem);
		itemRectangle.Width = (int)((float)itemRectangle.Width * adjustedItemScale);
		itemRectangle.Height = (int)((float)itemRectangle.Height * adjustedItemScale);
		if (direction == -1)
		{
			itemRectangle.X -= itemRectangle.Width;
		}
		if (gravDir == 1f)
		{
			itemRectangle.Y -= itemRectangle.Height;
		}
		if (sItem.useStyle == 1)
		{
			if ((double)itemAnimation < (double)itemAnimationMax * 0.333)
			{
				if (direction == -1)
				{
					itemRectangle.X -= (int)((double)itemRectangle.Width * 1.4 - (double)itemRectangle.Width);
				}
				itemRectangle.Width = (int)((double)itemRectangle.Width * 1.4);
				itemRectangle.Y += (int)((double)itemRectangle.Height * 0.5 * (double)gravDir);
				itemRectangle.Height = (int)((double)itemRectangle.Height * 1.1);
			}
			else if (!((double)itemAnimation < (double)itemAnimationMax * 0.666))
			{
				if (direction == 1)
				{
					itemRectangle.X -= (int)((double)itemRectangle.Width * 1.2);
				}
				itemRectangle.Width *= 2;
				itemRectangle.Y -= (int)(((double)itemRectangle.Height * 1.4 - (double)itemRectangle.Height) * (double)gravDir);
				itemRectangle.Height = (int)((double)itemRectangle.Height * 1.4);
			}
		}
		else
		{
			if (sItem.useStyle != 3)
			{
				return;
			}
			if ((double)itemAnimation > (double)itemAnimationMax * 0.666)
			{
				dontAttack = true;
				return;
			}
			if (direction == -1)
			{
				itemRectangle.X -= (int)((double)itemRectangle.Width * 1.4 - (double)itemRectangle.Width);
			}
			itemRectangle.Width = (int)((double)itemRectangle.Width * 1.4);
			itemRectangle.Y += (int)((double)itemRectangle.Height * 0.6);
			itemRectangle.Height = (int)((double)itemRectangle.Height * 0.6);
			if (sItem.type == 946 || sItem.type == 4707)
			{
				itemRectangle.Height += 14;
				itemRectangle.Width -= 10;
				if (direction == -1)
				{
					itemRectangle.X += 10;
				}
			}
		}
	}

	private void ItemCheck_UseDemonHeart(Item sItem)
	{
		if (sItem.type == 3335 && itemAnimation > 0 && !extraAccessory && Main.expertMode && ItemTimeIsZero)
		{
			ApplyItemTime(sItem);
			extraAccessory = true;
			NetMessage.SendData(4, -1, -1, null, whoAmI);
		}
	}

	private void ItemCheck_UseMinecartPowerUp(Item sItem)
	{
		if (sItem.type == 5289 && itemAnimation > 0 && ItemTimeIsZero)
		{
			ApplyItemTime(sItem);
			if (!unlockedSuperCart)
			{
				unlockedSuperCart = true;
				NetMessage.SendData(4, -1, -1, null, whoAmI);
			}
			QuickSpawnItem(GetItemSource_OpenItem(5289), 3353);
		}
	}

	private void ItemCheck_UseArtisanLoaf(Item sItem)
	{
		if (sItem.type == 5326 && itemAnimation > 0 && !ateArtisanBread && ItemTimeIsZero)
		{
			ApplyItemTime(sItem);
			ateArtisanBread = true;
			NetMessage.SendData(4, -1, -1, null, whoAmI);
		}
	}

	private void ItemCheck_StartActualUse(Item sItem)
	{
		bool flag = sItem.type == 4711;
		if (sItem.pick > 0 || sItem.axe > 0 || sItem.hammer > 0 || flag)
		{
			toolTime = 1;
		}
		if (grappling[0] > -1)
		{
			pulley = false;
			pulleyDir = 1;
			if (controlRight)
			{
				direction = 1;
			}
			else if (controlLeft)
			{
				direction = -1;
			}
		}
		StartChanneling(sItem);
		attackCD = 0;
		ResetMeleeHitCooldowns();
		ApplyItemAnimation(sItem);
		bool flag2 = ItemID.Sets.SkipsInitialUseSound[sItem.type];
		if (sItem.UseSound != null && !flag2)
		{
			bool flag3 = sItem.useStyle == 5 || sItem.useStyle == 13 || sItem.shoot > 0;
			bool? flag4 = ItemID.Sets.NetUseSoundSync[sItem.type];
			if (flag4.HasValue)
			{
				flag3 = flag4.Value;
			}
			if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer && Main.netMode == 1 && flag3)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(152, -1, -1, null, whoAmI);
			}
			if (whoAmI == Main.myPlayer || !flag3)
			{
				SoundEngine.PlaySound(sItem.UseSound, base.Center, sItem.useSoundPitch);
			}
		}
	}

	private void ApplyPotionDelay(Item sItem)
	{
		if (sItem.type == 3001)
		{
			int minValue = 2400;
			int num = 4200;
			potionDelay = Main.rand.Next(minValue, num + 1);
			if (pStone)
			{
				potionDelay = (int)((float)potionDelay * PhilosopherStoneDurationMultiplier);
			}
			AddBuff(21, potionDelay);
		}
		else if (sItem.type == 227 || sItem.type == 126)
		{
			potionDelay = restorationDelayTime;
			AddBuff(21, potionDelay);
		}
		else if (sItem.type == 1912)
		{
			potionDelay = eggnogDelayTime;
			AddBuff(21, potionDelay);
			TryToResetHungerToNeutral();
		}
		else if (sItem.type == 5)
		{
			potionDelay = mushroomDelayTime;
			AddBuff(21, potionDelay);
			TryToResetHungerToNeutral();
		}
		else
		{
			potionDelay = potionDelayTime;
			AddBuff(21, potionDelay);
		}
	}

	private void ApplyLifeAndOrMana(Item item)
	{
		int num = item.healLife;
		int healMana = item.healMana;
		if (item.type == 3001)
		{
			int healLife = item.healLife;
			int num2 = 120;
			num = Main.rand.Next(healLife, num2 + 1);
			if (Main.myPlayer == whoAmI)
			{
				float num3 = Main.rand.NextFloat();
				int num4 = 0;
				if (num3 <= 0.1f)
				{
					num4 = 240;
				}
				else if (num3 <= 0.3f)
				{
					num4 = 120;
				}
				else if (num3 <= 0.6f)
				{
					num4 = 60;
				}
				if (num4 > 0)
				{
					SetImmuneTimeForAllTypes(num4);
				}
			}
		}
		statLife += num;
		statMana += healMana;
		if (statLife > statLifeMax2)
		{
			statLife = statLifeMax2;
		}
		if (statMana > statManaMax2)
		{
			statMana = statManaMax2;
		}
		if (num > 0 && Main.myPlayer == whoAmI)
		{
			HealEffect(num);
		}
		if (healMana > 0)
		{
			AddBuff(94, manaSickTime);
			if (Main.myPlayer == whoAmI)
			{
				ManaEffect(healMana);
			}
		}
	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T15:18:53.704Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key insight: In vanilla, `ItemCheck_StartActualUse` does NOT branch on useStyle for gameplay behavior much — it applies item animation, plays sound. The useStyle mostly affects: 
1. bodyFrame (player sprite row)
2. itemLocation/itemRotation (ApplyUseStyle — held item drawing)
3. Melee hitbox (useStyle 1 or 3 only → melee damage only for 1 and 3)
4. Sound sync behavior (useStyle 5/13/shoot>0 → net sync)

The actual usage behavior (potion/food/place/shoot) is driven by other fields: healLife, buffType, consumable, createTile, shoot, etc., in ItemCheck_OwnerOnlyCode. So the task's framing "Game.ts 使用分发按 useStyle 路由" — the gameplay behavior is largely item-field-driven, and useStyle drives the animation/pose.

So my main deliverables:
1. Game.ts use dispatch: route by useStyle for animation timing (useAnimation), not a universal 30t swing. For useStyle 4 (hold overhead, food/potions), use itemAnimationMax with the body frame 2; useStyle 5 → no swing animation (itemAnimation used only for placement timing); 9, 13-16 → per-vanilla.
2. Renderer drawUseStyle extension: 4/5/9/13/14/15/16 pose formulas 1:1.
3. useCombatWeapon default return family classification.
4. Inventory.ts FitsAmmoSlot vs Item.cs:1351-1367.

Now let me look at the current Game.ts code around 2087-2204 and 2616, plus Renderer.ts drawUseStyle.
```

</details>

22	/** 原版 item id → 功能画像 */

65	 *  返回 null = 无战斗语义（材料/家具/药水等，或走既有手写分支的弓）。
66	 *  注意：消耗型 aiStyle 2（手里剑等抛物线投掷）仍走 Game.thrownCombat，
67	 *  这里只接管爆炸物族（ai16）——判定顺序见 Game.useItem */
68	export function combatWeapon(def: CombatWeaponItemLike): CombatWeapon | null {
69	  const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
70	  if (vid < 0) return null;
71	  const c = itemCombat(vid);
72	  if (!c || c.useAmmo || c.ammo) return null; // 弓弩/弹药体系不在此分流
73	  const shoot = c.shoot ?? 0;
74	  const ai = shoot ? projectileData(shoot)?.aiStyle ?? -1 : -1;
75	  const base = {
76	    damage: c.damage ?? 1,
77	    knockback: c.knockBack ?? 3,
78	    useTime: c.useTime ?? c.useAnimation ?? 20,
79	    shootSpeed: c.shootSpeed ?? 8,
80	  };
81	  if (shoot) {
82	    if (c.consumable && c.noMelee) {
83	      // 消耗型：爆炸物族（手雷 28/炸弹 29/炸药 30 等 ai16）弹跳+引信；
84	      // ai2 抛物线投掷武器（手里剑）交回 thrownCombat
85	      if (ai === AI_GRENADE) return { kind: 'grenade', shoot, ...base };
86	      return null;
87	    }
88	    if (ai === AI_BOOMERANG) return { kind: 'boomerang', shoot, ...base };
89	    if (ai === AI_SPEAR || ai === AI_SHORTSWORD) return { kind: 'spear', shoot, ...base };
90	    if (ai === AI_YOYO) return { kind: 'yoyo', shoot, ...base };
91	    if (c.summon) {
92	      // 召唤武器（31 件）：按投射物 AI 分流哨兵（53/54/123 定点炮台）与随从（其余飞行/地面）
93	      return { kind: 'summon', shoot, sentry: ai === 53 || ai === 54 || ai === 123, ...base };
94	    }
95	    if (ai === AI_FLAIL) return { kind: 'flail', shoot, ...base };
96	    // ★ melee+shoot（附魔剑 989/波刃 190 等 46 件）：原版语义 = 挥砍为主 +
97	    // 挥击中射弹（Player.cs:42880：flag4 = itemAnimation>0 && ItemTimeIsZero →
98	    // ItemCheck_Shoot，useTime 节奏发弹）。此前落入 shot 兜底 = 变成"光束枪"
99	    // 完全不挥砍——语义错误。数值取 melee 分支，附带 shoot/shootSpeed 给挥击侧发射
100	    if (c.melee && !c.noMelee) {
101	      return {
102	        kind: 'melee', damage: base.damage, knockback: base.knockback, useTime: base.useTime,
103	        useAnimation: c.useAnimation ?? base.useTime, autoReuse: !!c.autoReuse,
104	        shoot, shootSpeed: base.shootSpeed,
105	      };
106	    }
107	    if (c.magic || c.mana) return { kind: 'magic', shoot, mana: c.mana ?? 0, ...base };
108	    // 其余 melee/ranged+shoot（附魔剑光束等）：直射弹兜底
109	    if (c.melee || c.ranged || c.noMelee) return { kind: 'shot', shoot, ...base };
110	    return null;
111	  }
112	  // 纯近战（剑等：melee 且非 noMelee；无 shootSpeed 语义）。
113	  // useAnimation=挥击动画时长 / useTime=复用冷却（Player.ItemCheck: itemAnimationMax 与 itemTime
114	  // 分立，1790 件 vi 武器两者不同——只回传 useTime 会让挥速虚快）
115	  if (c.melee && !c.noMelee) {
116	    return {
117	      kind: 'melee', damage: base.damage, knockback: base.knockback, useTime: base.useTime,
118	      useAnimation: c.useAnimation ?? base.useTime, autoReuse: !!c.autoReuse,
119	    };
120	  }
121	  return null;
122	}
123	
124	/** 旧判定（消耗型投掷武器）保留：shoot+consumable+noMelee 且无 useAmmo/ammo */
125	export function thrownCombat(def: CombatWeaponItemLike): { shoot: number; damage: number } | null {
126	  const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
127	  if (vid < 0) return null;
128	  const c = itemCombat(vid);
129	  if (!c?.shoot || !c.consumable || !c.noMelee || c.useAmmo || c.ammo) return null;
130	  return { shoot: c.shoot, damage: c.damage ?? 0 };
131	}
132	
133	/** aiStyle → 投射物重力/tick（ai2/16 抛物线 0.3；其余直飞 0）——AI_002/AI_016 实测值 */
134	export function projGravity(shoot: number): number {
135	  const ai = projectileData(shoot)?.aiStyle ?? -1;
136	  return ai === AI_THROWN || ai === AI_GRENADE ? 0.3 : 0;
137	}
138

2357	   *  ② destination-in 重画精灵本体 → alpha 恢复为精灵轮廓，裁掉①的方形

2359	   *  （GetColor 二次绘制语义，Main.cs:24986 / NPC.cs:94903） */
2360	  private tintCache = new Map<string, HTMLCanvasElement>();
2361	  private tintedSprite(
2362	    key: string, frame: { img: CanvasImageSource; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number }, color: number[],
2363	  ): HTMLCanvasElement {
2364	    const k = `${key}|${frame.sx},${frame.sy},${frame.sw},${frame.sh}|${color.join(',')}`;
2365	    let c = this.tintCache.get(k);
2366	    if (c) return c;
2367	    c = document.createElement('canvas');
2368	    c.width = Math.max(1, frame.sw);
2369	    c.height = Math.max(1, frame.sh);
2370	    const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
2371	    cx.drawImage(frame.img, frame.sx, frame.sy, frame.sw, frame.sh, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
2372	    // ① RGB 乘 color（multiply 的 alpha 取并集 → 透明区被铺色，下一步裁掉）
2373	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
2374	    cx.fillStyle = `rgb(${color[0]},${color[1]},${color[2]})`;
2375	    cx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
2376	    // ② 用精灵本体当 destination-in 的源：alpha 恢复为轮廓形状
2377	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
2378	    cx.drawImage(frame.img, frame.sx, frame.sy, frame.sw, frame.sh, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
2379	    cx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
2380	    if (this.tintCache.size > 1024) this.tintCache.clear();
2381	    this.tintCache.set(k, c);
2382	    return c;
2383	  }

	private void ItemCheck_ApplyManaRegenDelay(Item sItem)
	{
		if (!spaceGun || (sItem.type != 127 && sItem.type != 4347 && sItem.type != 4348 && sItem.type != 514))
		{
			manaRegenDelay = (int)maxRegenDelay;
		}
	}

	public Vector2 GetFrontHandPosition(CompositeArmStretchAmount stretch, float rotation)
	{
		float num = rotation + MathF.PI / 2f;
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2((float)Math.Cos(num), (float)Math.Sin(num));
		switch (stretch)
		{
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.Full:
			vector *= 10f;
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.None:
			vector *= 4f;
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.Quarter:
			vector *= 6f;
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.ThreeQuarters:
			vector *= 8f;
			break;
		}
		if (direction == -1)
		{
			vector += new Vector2(4f, -2f);
			vector += new Vector2(0f, -3f).RotatedBy(rotation + MathF.PI / 2f);
		}
		else
		{
			vector += new Vector2(-4f, -2f);
			vector += new Vector2(0f, 3f).RotatedBy(rotation + MathF.PI / 2f);
		}
		return MountedCenter + vector;
	}

	public Vector2 GetBackHandPosition(CompositeArmStretchAmount stretch, float rotation)
	{
		float num = rotation + MathF.PI / 2f;
		Vector2 vector = new Vector2((float)Math.Cos(num), (float)Math.Sin(num));
		switch (stretch)
		{
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.Full:
			vector *= new Vector2(10f, 12f);
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.None:
			vector *= new Vector2(4f, 6f);
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.Quarter:
			vector *= new Vector2(6f, 8f);
			break;
		case CompositeArmStretchAmount.ThreeQuarters:
			vector *= new Vector2(8f, 10f);
			break;
		}
		if (direction == -1)
		{
			vector += new Vector2(-6f, -2f);
		}
		else
		{
			vector += new Vector2(6f, -2f);
		}
		return MountedCenter + vector;
	}

For dir=1: offX_from_center = fw/2 - 4 - num18. rot = 0.8*dir → 0.8. Body row 3. These are umbrella items (holdStyle 2) — actually umbrella is a holdStyle item mostly used while falling; the use action toggles open. Only 2 items; implement formula anyway (cheap).

**useStyle 13 (shortsword)**: body 3-stage like 1; itemLocation from composite arm with rotation = itemRotation (aim). 
```
useAnimation = itemTimeMax || itemAnimationMax || sItem.useAnimation
num24 = 1 - (itemAnimation % useAnimation)/useAnimation   ← pAnim-like
stretch tiers: <=0.33 Quarter; <=0.66 ThreeQuarters; else Full
rotation = itemRotation*dir... - π/2*dir → but itemRotation = atan2(aimY*dir, aimX*dir); vanilla: itemRotation * Directions.Y (gravDir=1) - (π/2)*dir
SetCompositeArmFront(stretch, rotation)
```
Note: useStyle 13 doesn't set itemLocation in ApplyUseStyle! It only sets composite arm. The item location then comes from PlayerDrawLayers using... hmm, when SetCompositeArmFront is set, the draw code positions the item at the front hand position (PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3863+ uses compFrontArm). So itemLocation = GetFrontHandPosition(stretch, rotation).

For dir=1: rotation = itemRotation - π/2 where itemRotation = atan2(aimY, aimX). GetFrontHandPosition(stretch, rotation):
num = rotation + π/2 = itemRotation. vector = (cos(itemRotation), sin(itemRotation)) * mult (Full=10, ThreeQuarters=8, Quarter=6, None=4) + (-4,-2) + (0,3).RotatedBy(itemRotation) = mult*(cosθ, sinθ) + (-4 - 3 sinθ, -2 + 3 cosθ).
offX = that X, offY = p.h/2 + that Y. Draw rotation: itemRotation itself? The item drawn in composite-arm mode is drawn with itemRotation. So rot = itemRotation (dir=1 space).

**useStyle 14**: rot 0; itemLocation = Center + (6*dir, 40 - 8 + headgearOffset) → offX=6, offY = 40 - 8 = 32 + headgear offset. OffsetsPlayerHeadgear[bodyFrame.Y/56] — a per-row offset table. Main.OffsetsPlayerHeadgear — I'd need values. It's a Vector2 array indexed by frame row. Hmm — for 1 item (Nightglow, magic weapon). I can approximate headgear offset as (0,0) or implement the table. Let me check the table values in Main.cs.

**useStyle 15**: offX=6, offY = 30 - 2 = 28 + headgear. Scrying Orb (5644). Also holdStyle 8 same formula (that's the idle pose for the same item! interesting: holdStyle 8 = same as useStyle 15 pose).

**useStyle 16**: delegate to HoldStyle (item 856 Unicorn on a Stick, holdStyle 1) → same as holdStyle 1 static: offX = fw/2 + 2 (holdStyle 1 default), offY = 24; special 856: X = width*0.5 + 4*dir → offX=4, Y = position.Y + 34 → offY=34.

Let me get OffsetsPlayerHeadgear values. grep Main.cs.
````

161	  /** 显示用部位贴图：时装格（armor[10-12]）优先，回落装备格（Player.DisplayDoll 语义） */
162	  displayArmor(): Array<number | null> {
163	    return [0, 1, 2].map((k) => this.armor[VANITY_ARMOR_START + k]?.id ?? this.armor[k]?.id ?? null);
164	  }
165	
166	  /** armor[20] 下标能否接收物品（ItemSlot.PickItemMovementAction :1363-1494）：
167	   *  0-2/10-12 部位校验（head/body/leg slot）；3-9 功能配饰（vanity 排除）/13-19 配饰（含社交）；
168	   *  染料走 dye 数组。vi_ 批次物品的部位/配饰归属查 vanilla-itemstats.json（注册期无字段） */
169	  armorAccepts(i: number, id: number): boolean {
170	    if (i < 0 || i >= ARMOR_TOTAL) return false;
171	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[id];
172	    if (!d) return false;
173	    const part = i % VANITY_ARMOR_START; // 0-2=部位 3-9=配饰（与 10-19 镜像同判）
174	    if (part <= 2) {
175	      if (d.armor) return d.armor.slot === (part as ArmorSlot);
176	      return armorSlotIndexOfInternal(id) === part;
177	    }
178	    if (d.accessory) return i < VANITY_ARMOR_START; // legacy 配饰（社交槽放宽）
179	    if (i < VANITY_ARMOR_START) return statOfInternal(id)?.acc === 1; // 功能配饰槽：vanity 排除
180	    return statOfInternal(id)?.acc !== undefined; // 社交配饰槽：功能/社交都收
181	  }
182	
183	  /** dye 槽能否接收（ItemSlot context 12/25/27/33：checkItem.dye > 0——染料物品白名单） */
184	  dyeAccepts(id: number): boolean {
185	    return isDyeItem(id);
186	  }
187	
188	  /** miscEquips 槽能否接收（ItemSlot context 16-20：宠物/光源/矿车/坐骑/钩爪分类门） */
189	  miscAccepts(slot: number, id: number): boolean {
190	    return miscSlotAccepts(slot, id);
191	  }
192	
193	  /** slots 下标能否接收物品（钱币/弹药专格校验；0-49 通用） */
194	  canAccept(i: number, id: number): boolean {
195	    if (isCoinSlot(i)) return coinSlotAccepts(i, id);
196	    if (isAmmoSlot(i)) return ammoSlotAccepts(id);
197	    return true;
198	  }
199	
200	  /** 添加物品（Player.GetItem :38657-38736 优先级 1:1），返回未能放入的数量：
201	   *  ① 钱币先进 50-53；② 弹药进 54-57（先合并已有再空格）；
202	   *  ③ 合并 0-49 已有堆（先于空槽）；④ 空槽【降序】从背包末尾填（原版非收藏物降序）
203	   *  ⑤ 钱币满 100 自动合大（DoCoins :38573-38589） */
204	  add(id: number, count: number): number {
205	    const max = ITEM_DEFS[id].maxStack;
206	    // ① 钱币（合并→空格→DoCoins）
207	    const ci = coinIndexOf(id);
208	    if (ci >= 0) {
209	      count = this.fillRange(COIN_START, COIN_SLOTS, id, count, max);
210	      if (count > 0) count = this.doCoinsFromSlot(COIN_START, COIN_SLOTS, ci, id, count, max);
211	      return count;
212	    }
213	    // ② 弹药（FillAmmo :38591-38655：先合并 54-57 已有再空格）
214	    if (ammoSlotAccepts(id)) {
215	      count = this.fillRange(AMMO_START, AMMO_SLOTS, id, count, max);
216	    }
217	    // ③ 合并 0-49 已有堆
218	    count = this.fillRange(0, BAG_SIZE, id, count, max);
219	    // ④ 空槽降序（原版非 favorited 降序从背包尾填，:38711-38730）
220	    for (let i = BAG_SIZE - 1; i >= 0 && count > 0; i--) {
221	      if (!this.slots[i]) {
222	        const put = Math.min(max, count);
223	        this.slots[i] = { id, stack: put };
224	        count -= put;
225	      }
226	    }
227	    return count;
228	  }
229	
230	  /** 范围内先合并后空格填充 */
231	  private fillRange(start: number, len: number, id: number, count: number, max: number): number {
232	    for (let i = start; i < start + len && count > 0; i++) {
233	      const s = this.slots[i];
234	      if (s && s.id === id && s.stack < max) {
235	        const put = Math.min(max - s.stack, count);
236	        s.stack += put;
237	        count -= put;
238	      }
239	    }
240	    for (let i = start; i < start + len && count > 0; i++) {
241	      if (!this.slots[i]) {
242	        const put = Math.min(max, count);
243	        this.slots[i] = { id, stack: put };
244	        count -= put;
245	      }
246	    }
247	    return count;
248	  }
249	
250	  /** DoCoins（Player.cs:38573-38589）：同币 stack==100 → 升一级面额并在 0-53 找同类未满堆 +1，递归 */
251	  private doCoinsFromSlot(start: number, len: number, ci: number, id: number, count: number, max: number): number {
252	    void start; void len; void ci; void max;
253	    // 100 铜→银（coin_copper→coin_silver）等：仅当该面额格已达 100
254	    for (let tier = 0; tier < 3; tier++) {
255	      const s = this.slots[COIN_START + tier];
256	      if (s && s.id === ITEM_BY_KEY[COIN_KEYS[tier]] && s.stack >= 100) {
257	        this.slots[COIN_START + tier] = null;
258	        // 升级币放入钱币格（递归走 add 保持链）
259	        const left = this.add(ITEM_BY_KEY[COIN_KEYS[tier + 1]], 1);
260	        if (left > 0) return count; // 放不下（理论不发生）
261	      }
262	    }
263	    return count;
264	  }
265	
266	  removeAt(i: number, count = 1): boolean {
267	    const s = this.slots[i];
268	    if (!s || s.stack < count) return false;
269	    s.stack -= count;
270	    if (s.stack <= 0) this.slots[i] = null;
271	    return true;
272	  }
273	
274	  countOf(id: number): number {
275	    let n = 0;
276	    for (const s of this.slots) if (s && s.id === id) n += s.stack;
277	    return n;
278	  }
279	
280	  /** 全格扫扣（0-57；不动 armor/dye/trash） */
281	  remove(id: number, count: number): boolean {
282	    if (this.countOf(id) < count) return false;
283	    for (let i = 0; i < this.slots.length && count > 0; i++) {
284	      const s = this.slots[i];
285	      if (s && s.id === id) {
286	        const take = Math.min(s.stack, count);
287	        s.stack -= take;
288	        count -= take;
289	        if (s.stack <= 0) this.slots[i] = null;
290	      }
291	    }
292	    return true;
293	  }
294	
295	  /** 交换/合并 slots 两格（UI 拖拽；钱币/弹药专格校验） */
296	  swap(i: number, j: number) {
297	    const a = this.slots[i], b = this.slots[j];
298	    if (a && !this.canAccept(j, a.id)) return;
299	    if (b && !this.canAccept(i, b.id)) return;
300	    if (a && b && a.id === b.id) {
301	      const max = ITEM_DEFS[a.id].maxStack;
302	      const put = Math.min(max - b.stack, a.stack);
303	      b.stack += put;
304	      a.stack -= put;
305	      if (a.stack <= 0) this.slots[i] = null;
306	    } else {
307	      this.slots[i] = b;
308	      this.slots[j] = a;
309	    }
310	  }
311	
312	  /** 旧布局（≤53 槽 + 盔甲/时装/配饰内嵌）→ 新布局迁移（读档调用） */
313	  static migrateLegacy(legacy: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>): {
314	    slots: Array<InvItem | null>; armor: Array<InvItem | null>;
315	  } {
316	    const inv = new Inventory();
317	    if (legacy.length <= LEGACY_COIN_START + 3 && legacy.length > 0) {
318	      // 旧 54 槽布局：0-39 背包原位 / 40-44 配饰→armor[3-7] / 45-47 盔甲→armor[0-2]
319	      // / 48-50 时装→armor[10-12] / 51-53 钱币→slots[50-52]（铂金格留空）
320	      for (let i = 0; i < 40 && i < legacy.length; i++) inv.slots[i] = legacy[i] ? { ...legacy[i]! } : null;
321	      for (let k = 0; k < 5; k++) inv.armor[ACC_ARMOR_START + k] = legacy[LEGACY_ACCESSORY_START + k] ? { ...legacy[LEGACY_ACCESSORY_START + k]! } : null;
322	      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) inv.armor[k] = legacy[LEGACY_ARMOR_START + k] ? { ...legacy[LEGACY_ARMOR_START + k]! } : null;
323	      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) inv.armor[VANITY_ARMOR_START + k] = legacy[LEGACY_VANITY_START + k] ? { ...legacy[LEGACY_VANITY_START + k]! } : null;
324	      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) inv.slots[COIN_START + k] = legacy[LEGACY_COIN_START + k] ? { ...legacy[LEGACY_COIN_START + k]! } : null;
325	    } else {
326	      // 新布局直读
327	      for (let i = 0; i < INV_SIZE && i < legacy.length; i++) inv.slots[i] = legacy[i] ? { ...legacy[i]! } : null;
328	    }
329	    return { slots: inv.slots, armor: inv.armor };
330	  }
331	}

{

}

// vi_ 道具功能桥接回归（工具/药水）：vanilla-itemfunc 数据 + Buff 映射链路
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { itemFuncOfVid } from '../src/data/vanillaItemFunc';
import { BUFF_DEFS, BuffType } from '../src/stats/Buffs';

// 原版 buff id → BuffType（Game 桥接同款反查）
const BUFF_BY_VANILLA = new Map<number, BuffType>();
for (const [bt, d] of Object.entries(BUFF_DEFS)) BUFF_BY_VANILLA.set(d.vanillaBuff, Number(bt) as BuffType);

describe('vanilla-itemfunc 数据（1456 Item.cs 提取）', () => {
  it('铁镐 1：pick 40 / damage 5 / melee', () => {
    const f = itemFuncOfVid(1)!;
    expect(f.pick).toBe(40);
    expect(f.damage).toBe(5);
    expect(f.melee).toBe(1);
  });
  it('铁锤 7：hammer 40；钴镐 77：pick 110？——抽铜镐 3509：pick 35', () => {
    expect(itemFuncOfVid(7)!.hammer).toBe(40);
    expect(itemFuncOfVid(3509)!.pick).toBe(35);
  });
  it('治疗药水 188 healLife 100 / 魔力药水 110 healMana 50', () => {
    expect(itemFuncOfVid(188)!.healLife).toBe(100);
    expect(itemFuncOfVid(188)!.potion).toBe(1);
    expect(itemFuncOfVid(110)!.healMana).toBe(50);
  });
  it('Buff 药水：铁皮 292 buffType 5+28800t（8 分钟）/ 挖矿 2322 buffType 104+36000t', () => {
    const iron = itemFuncOfVid(292)!;
    expect(iron.buffType).toBe(5);
    expect(iron.buffTime).toBe(28800);
    expect(iron.consumable).toBe(1);
    const mine = itemFuncOfVid(2322)!;
    expect(mine.buffType).toBe(104);
    expect(mine.buffTime).toBe(36000);
  });
});

describe('桥接链路（Game.itemFuncTool / 药水分支同源判定）', () => {
  it('工具派生公式（itemFuncTool 同款）：铁镐 → pick 40%', () => {
    const pick = itemFuncOfVid(1)!;
    const derived = {
      type: pick.pick ? 'pick' : pick.axe ? 'axe' : 'hammer',
      power: pick.pick ?? pick.axe ?? pick.hammer,
    };
    expect(derived).toEqual({ type: 'pick', power: 40 });
  });
  it('BUFF_BY_VANILLA 反查：5→铁皮 / 3→敏捷 / 2→恢复 / 8→羽落 / 15→水上行走', () => {
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(5)).toBe(BuffType.Ironskin);
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(3)).toBe(BuffType.Agility);
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(2)).toBe(BuffType.Regen);
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(8)).toBe(BuffType.Featherfall);
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(15)).toBe(BuffType.WaterWalking);
  });
  it('耐药性 21 不作为主动施加的药水 Buff（治疗副作用另行施加）', () => {
    // 药水分支对 buffType=21（PotionSickness）不主动 apply——它由治疗动作触发
    expect(BUFF_BY_VANILLA.get(21)).toBe(BuffType.Resistance);
  });
});

import { itemCombat } from '../data/vanillaItemCombat';
import { itemFuncOfVid } from '../data/vanillaItemFunc';
// PaintOrCoating（Item.cs:334：paint/paintCoating>0）——提取表未含 paint 字段，
// 集合取 Item.cs paint = 1..30 段（1073-1099 涂料 + 1966-1968/4668/5344 涂层）
const PAINT_OR_COATING = new Set([...]);

export function ammoSlotAccepts(id) {
  const d = ITEM_DEFS[id]; if (!d) return false;
  const vid = viIdFromKey(d.key);
  if (vid < 0) return d.ammoId !== undefined && d.ammoId > 0; // legacy（木箭/燃烧箭 ammoId 40）
  // Item.FitsAmmoSlot 1:1（Item.cs:1351-1367）
  if (vid === 353) return true;                       // 信号枪本体（ammo=353+notAmmo 豁免）
  const f = itemFuncOfVid(vid);
  if (f?.notAmmo) return false;
  const c = itemCombat(vid);
  if ((c?.ammo ?? 0) > 0) return true;
  if ((f?.bait ?? 0) > 0) return true;
  if (vid === 530 || vid === 849) return true;        // 导线/油漆喷枪? exemptions
  if (PAINT_OR_COATING.has(vid)) return true;
  return false;
}

import projJson from './vanilla-projectiles.json';

export interface ProjectileData {
  aiStyle?: number;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  penetrate?: number;
  timeLeft?: number;
  damage?: number;
  light?: number;
  friendly?: boolean;
  melee?: boolean;
  ranged?: boolean;
  magic?: boolean;
  minion?: boolean;
  hostile?: boolean;
  tileCollide?: boolean;

 "new_string": "/** 弹药格校验（Item.FitsAmmoSlot 1:1，Item.cs:1351-1367）：\n *  ```csharp\n *  if ((type != 0 && ammo <= 0 && bait <= 0 && type != 530 && type != 849 && !PaintOrCoating) || notAmmo)\n *      return type == 353;\n *  return true;\n *  ```\n *  即：vid==353（信号枪本体，ammo=353+notAmmo 双设但豁免）恒收；\n *  其余 = (ammo>0 || bait>0 || vid∈{530,849} || 涂料) 且非 notAmmo。\n *  530=导线、849=油漆喷枪用填充物的原版硬编码豁免。\n *  PaintOrCoating（Item.cs:334：paint/paintCoating>0）提取表无 paint 字段，\n *  集合取 Item.cs paint=1..30 的 case 段（1073-1099 涂料 + 1966-1968/4668/5344 涂层） */\nconst PAINT_OR_COATING = new Set([\n  ...Array.from({ length: 1099 - 1073 + 1 }, (_, i) => 1073 + i),\n  1966, 1967, 1968, 4668, 5344,\n]);\n\nexport function ammoSlotAccepts(id: number): boolean {\n  const d = ITEM_DEFS[id];\n  if (!d) return false;\n  const vid = d.vid ?? viIdFromKey(d.key);\n  if (vid < 0) return d.ammoId !== undefined && d.ammoId > 0; // legacy 注册表自带 ammoId\n  if (vid === 353) return true;\n  const f = itemFuncOfVid(vid);\n  if (f?.notAmmo) return false;\n  if (vid === 530 || vid === 849 || PAINT_OR_COATING.has(vid)) return true;\n  if ((f?.bait ?? 0) > 0) return true;\n  return (itemCombat(vid)?.ammo ?? 0) > 0;\n}"
}

// 物品注册表
export type ToolType = 'pick' | 'axe' | 'sword' | 'hammer';
// Buff 类型（药水饮用后生效）
export type PotionBuff = 'agility' | 'ironskin' | 'resistance' | 'thorns' | 'regen'
  | 'obsidianSkin' | 'gills' | 'manaRegen' | 'magicPower' | 'featherfall' | 'waterWalking' | 'archery' | 'nightOwl';
// 盔甲部位：0 头盔 1 胸甲 2 胫甲（与 Inventory.armorSlotReq 的格位对应）
export type ArmorSlot = 0 | 1 | 2;

export interface ItemDef {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  maxStack: number;
  tile?: string;          // 放置的 tile key
  placeStyle?: number;     // 放置的样式索引(vanilla style 帧 X 偏移 = style*fw*18)

I referenced `rangedAmmoWeapon(heldDef)` — need to add that helper in vanillaItemCombat.ts: returns the useAmmo id for vi_ ranged weapons (useAmmo>0) excluding... Actually combatWeapon returns null for useAmmo items. A vi_ item with useAmmo is a ranged weapon. Define:

```ts
/** vi_ useAmmo 远程武器（枪/发射器，DefaultToRangedWeapon 族）：返回 useAmmo id；
 *  非 useAmmo 物品返回 0（战斗武器/弹药/其它不在此列）。Game 远程分支据此接管
 *  combatWeapon 按 useAmmo 排除的枪族（此前静默失效） */
export function rangedAmmoWeapon(def: CombatWeaponItemLike): number {
  const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);
  if (vid < 0) return 0;
  const ua = itemCombat(vid)?.useAmmo ?? 0;
  return ua > 0 ? ua : 0;
}
```

Earlier branches: tools/sword-cw (melee), pick/axe/hammer, extractinator, wireTool, wallId, tile, generic-block(!). The generic block at 2525 catches everything not tool & cw.kind!=='summon' — including vi_ guns! It sets a swing and falls through (doesn't return), then the else-if chain: potion → ... → ranged. Since the generic block is a separate `if` (not part of the chain), the chain still evaluates. Order OK: ranged branch will fire for guns (useTime===0 required — the generic block doesn't set useTime, so ok).

Hmm wait — one problem: the generic block sets `this.useSwing(heldDef)` for guns too (useStyle 5), then the ranged branch sets another swing with aim — the second overwrites. Fine.

Another consideration: bows (legacy wooden_bow with def.ranged) — do they hit the generic block first? wooden_bow has no tool, cw? combatWeapon(39): 39 has useAmmo(40) → null. So cw null → generic block runs → sets swing, no consume; then ranged branch fires ✓ (as before, previously it set 30t swing then fired bow without swing... previously the block set a 30t swing with useStyle undefined → now useSwing gives useStyle 5 with aim undefined (useSwing only sets aim when us===5||13 — for bows us IS 5 → aim = ang... wait in the generic block I call `this.useSwing(heldDef)` without aim → aim undefined. Then the ranged branch calls useSwing(heldDef, ang) → aim set ✓).

 "new_string": "  /** 手持物 useStyle 分族（Player.cs:49939 ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle switch）：\n   *  vi_ → vanilla-itemfunc.useStyle（2141 件分布 1:1504 / 4:74 / 5:243 / 9:70 …）；\n   *  legacy 药水无 vid → 9（Item.DefaultToHealingPotion：useStyle=9 举杯）；\n   *  其余 legacy 缺数据 → 1（挥砍）。返回值供 Renderer 姿势/身体行路由 */\n  private heldUseStyle(def: (typeof ITEM_DEFS)[number] | null): number {\n    if (!def) return 1;\n    const vid = def.vid ?? viIdFromKey(def.key);\n    if (vid >= 0) return itemFuncOfVid(vid)?.useStyle ?? 1;\n    if (def.potion) return 9;\n    // legacy 无 vid 的原版 4 族（Boss 召唤物/生命水晶，Item.cs SetDefaults useStyle=4）\n    return LEGACY_USE_STYLE[def.key] ?? 1;\n  }"

2273	        const h = 56;
2274	        const w = (frame.sw / frame.sh) * h;

2285	    if (!drawn) {

2289	      const frame = p.frame;

2292	
2293	    ctx.restore();
2294	    // 使用类物品（托举：药水/方块等非工具）：身体前方图层，避免被身体挡住
2295	    if (swing && swing.item >= 0 && !ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.tool) {
2296	      this.drawUseItem(ctx, p, swing);
2297	    }

2299	    // 气口气泡：头部浸水时显示（在朝向变换外绘制——左右移动不镜像），
2300	    // 随气量消散；整体更透明
2301	    if (p.headUnderwater || p.breath < 5 || p.refillT >= 0) {
2302	      const gap = 12.5; // 间距 12.5px，气泡直径 11px，留 ~1.5px 视觉分隔
2303	      const baseX = p.cx - (5 * gap) / 2 + gap / 2, baseY = p.y - p.h * 0.4 - 14;
2304	      // 优先原版 Bubble.png（22×22，不透明），程序化圆仅兜底
2305	      const bub = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vmisc('vanilla/Bubble.png') : null;
2306	      // 平滑气量 = (气口-1) + 当前正在消耗那颗的剩余比例 → 逐个渐隐（不画已耗尽的）。
2307	      // 直接 breath+drain 会在扣气瞬间把气泡重新顶满一格
2308	      // 水下：平滑消耗（当前颗渐隐）；出水：0.8s 快速补满动画
2309	      let display: number;
2310	      if (p.refillT >= 0) {
2311	        // 从余量处起填：refillFrom + 缺口 × 进度（不从第一颗重新冒）
2312	        display = p.refillFrom + (5 - p.refillFrom) * p.refill01;
2313	      } else {
2314	        const drain = p.headUnderwater && p.breath > 0 ? p.breathDrain01 : 1;
2315	        display = p.breath - 1 + drain;
2316	      }
2317	      // 5 个槽位固定占位（消耗时槽位不挪动，只是该槽的气泡渐隐消失）
2318	      for (let b = 0; b < 5; b++) {
2319	        const v = display - b; // 该颗的可见度：≥1 满，0..1 渐隐，≤0 跳过
2320	        if (v <= 0) continue;
2321	        const bx = baseX + b * gap;
2322	        const by = baseY;
2323	        ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, v);
2324	        if (bub) {
2325	          ctx.drawImage(bub.img, bub.sx, bub.sy, bub.sw, bub.sh, bx - 5.5, by - 5.5, 11, 11);
2326	        } else {
2327	          ctx.fillStyle = '#BFE3FF';
2328	          ctx.beginPath();
2329	          ctx.arc(bx, by, 4.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2330	          ctx.fill();
2331	          ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
2332	          ctx.beginPath();
2333	          ctx.arc(bx - 1.5, by - 1.5, 1.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
2334	          ctx.fill();

2336	      }
2337	      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

2343	  /** 使用/挥舞动画（与静持同尺寸：原始像素比例 ×0.9，不放大）。
2344	   *  工具/武器：绕持握点旋转挥砍（-63° → +57° 完整弧，身后图层）。

2346	   *  后半程沿同一路径转回（三角波往返），收尾回到起始位——
2347	   *  不会继续向前转导致"放下时飘到身前一格"。
2348	   *  图层：工具/武器挥砍在身体之前调用（身后），使用类托举在身体之后调用（身前） */

2352	    // 原版 useStyle=1（ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle Player.cs:49939-50108）1:1：
2353	    // p = itemAnimation/itemAnimationMax（起 1 收 0）——swing.t 从 dur 递减到 0，同向
2354	    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));
2355	    // itemRotation = (p-0.5)*(-dir)*3.5 - dir*0.3（:50105）——约 200° 线性挥砍弧
2356	    const rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;
2357	    // itemLocation 三段（早 p>2/3 / 中 1/3-2/3 / 晚 <1/3），X 档位按帧宽、Y 按帧高
2358	    const fw = ar ? ar.sw : 14, fh = ar ? ar.sh : 14;
2359	    // X 档位（:49948 早 / :49990 中 / :50038 晚，各自独立阈值表）
2360	    const xTier = (w: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0
2361	      ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 14 : 10
2362	      : phase === 1
2363	        ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 18 : 10
2364	        : w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w >= 48 ? 18 : w > 32 ? 14 : 6;
2365	    // Y 档位：早=顶+24（:49958）；中按帧高（:50000）；晚按帧高（:50078）
2366	    const yTier = (h: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0 ? 24
2367	      : h > 64 ? 14 : h > 52 ? 12 : phase === 1 && h > 32 ? 8 : 10;
2368	    let offX: number, offY: number;
2369	    if (pAnim > 2 / 3) {
2370	      offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 0);
2371	      offY = yTier(fh, 0);
2372	    } else if (pAnim > 1 / 3) {
2373	      offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 1);
2374	      offY = yTier(fh, 1);
2375	    } else {

2377	      offX = -(fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 2));
2378	      offY = yTier(fh, 2);
2379	    }
2380	    ctx.save();

2383	    ctx.rotate(rot);

2388	      // 此前误画在 (fw/2,−fh)：武器整把向外飘半个身位宽 = "离手很远"的根因
2389	      ctx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, 0, -fh, ar.sw, ar.sh);
2390	    } else if (icon) {

2392	    }

3 : 2;\n      }\n      case 5: {\n        // :35986-36005：默认行3；itemRotation*dir < -0.75 → 行2、> 0.6 → 行4\n        const n21 = (swing.aim ?? 0) * p.facing;\n        if (n21 < -0.75) return 2;\n        if (n21 > 0.6) return 4;\n        return 3;\n      }\n      case 14:\n      case 15:\n      case 10:\n      case 16:\n        return -1;\n      default:\n        return pAnim > 2 / 3 ? 1 : pAnim > 1 / 3 ? 2 : 3;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 使用/挥舞动画（与静持同尺寸：原始像素比例，不放大）。\n   *  useStyle 1:1 分族（Player.cs:49916 ItemCheck_ApplyUseStyle switch）：\n   *   1 挥砍三段（已验证）/ 2 咀嚼 / 3 雨伞 / 4 举过头 / 5 朝鼠标静持（法杖钉中心）\n   *   6 饮用旋转 / 9 举杯（DefaultToHealingPotion）/ 13 短剑突刺（复合手臂）/\n   *   14-15 头侧持 / 16 委托静持（holdStyle）。\n   *  坐标约定：全部按 direction=+1 推导（offX 正=身前），朝左靠镜像空间翻转；\n   *  offY 从 p.y（碰撞盒顶）起算；旋转 origin = 底角（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3260）。\n   *  图层：工具/武器挥砍在身体之前调用（身后），使用类托举在身体之后调用（身前） */\n  private drawUseItem(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, p: Player, swing: { t: number; dur: number; item: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number }) {\n    const ar = this.atlasIcon(swing.item);\n    const icon = ar ? null : this.itemIcon(swing.item);\n    const pAnim = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, swing.t / swing.dur));\n    const fw = ar ? ar.sw : 14, fh = ar ? ar.sh : 14;\n    const us = swing.useStyle ?? 1;\n    // → { rot, offX, offY }（dir=1 空间）；hide = 原版把 itemLocation 挪到 -1000（不画）\n    let rot = 0, offX = 0, offY = 0, hide = false;\n    switch (us) {\n      case 1: {\n        // useStyle=1（:49939-50108）1:1：约 200° 线性挥砍弧 + 三段持位\n        rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;\n        const xTier = (w: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0\n          ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 14 : 10\n          : phase === 1\n            ? w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w > 32 ? 18 : 10\n            : w >= 92 ? 38 : w >= 64 ? 28 : w >= 52 ? 24 : w >= 48 ? 18 : w > 32 ? 14 : 6;\n        const yTier = (h: number, phase: 0 | 1 | 2) => phase === 0 ? 24\n          : h > 64 ? 14 : h > 52 ? 12 : phase === 1 && h > 32 ? 8 : 10;\n        if (pAnim > 2 / 3) { offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 0); offY = yTier(fh, 0); }\n        else if (pAnim > 1 / 3) { offX = fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 1); offY = yTier(fh, 1); }\n        else { offX = -(fw * 0.5 - xTier(fw, 2)); offY = yTier(fh, 2); }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 2: {\n        // 咀嚼（:50183-50224，DefaultToFood 非 gulp 族：蘑菇 5/蜂蜜鳍 2314）：\n        // 手臂伸缩档 num15=8/6/4/2 → offX = num15+10-fw/2，Y = 中心 + fh/2-4；rot 不变\n        const n14 = 1 - pAnim;\n        const n15 = n14 <= 0.25 ? 8 : n14 <= 0.5 ? 6 : n14 <= 0.75 ? 4 : 2;\n        offX = n15 + 10 - fw * 0.5;\n        offY = p.h * 0.5 + fh * 0.5 - 4;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 3: {\n        // 雨伞（:50279-50312）：收势段（pAnim>0.666）itemLocation=-1000 不画\n        if (pAnim > 0.666) { hide = true; break; }\n        rot = 0.8;\n        offX = fw * 0.5 - 4 - Math.max(pAnim * fw * 1.2 - 10, 8);\n        offY = 24;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 4: {\n        // 举过头（:50313-50338）：rot=0；offX = fw/2-13（3601/5114/5120 再 -10）\n        // offY = fh/2+4（5114 再 -2）——使用中身体行 2（双手举过头顶）\n        offX = fw * 0.5 - 13;\n        offY = fh * 0.5 + 4;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 5: {\n        // 朝鼠标静持（:50339-50422 + :46708 itemRotation=瞄准角）：\n        // 法杖族钉 MountedCenter+瞄准向量×6；默认 X=中心-2*dir、Y=中心-fh/2\n        rot = swing.aim ?? 0;\n        const vid = viIdFromKey(ITEM_DEFS[swing.item]?.key ?? '');\n        if (vid >= 0 && STAFF_ITEMS.has(vid)) {\n          offX = Math.cos(rot) * 6;\n          offY = p.h * 0.5 + Math.sin(rot) * 6;\n        } else {\n          offX = -2;\n          offY = p.h * 0.5 - fh * 0.5;\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 6: {\n        // 饮用旋转（:50158-50182，回归药水 2350/4870）：与 7 同构反向\n        const n13 = Math.min((1 - pAnim) * 6, 1);\n        rot = (1 - n13) * 2 - 1.4;\n        if (n13 >= 0.5) { offX = fw * 0.5 - 9 - rot * 12; offY = 38 + rot * 4; }\n        else { offX = fw * 0.5 - 9 - rot * 16; offY = 38 + rot; }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 9: {\n        // 举杯（:50127-50157，DefaultToHealingPotion/DefaultToFood gulp）：\n        // t=clamp((1-pAnim)/0.7)；rot = -2t+0.7；\n        // 持位 = GetFrontHandPosition(Full, num11-π/2) 再缩放 Lerp(1.5,1.2,t)+前移 Lerp(8,2,t)\n        const t = Math.min(Math.max((1 - pAnim) / 0.7, 0), 1);\n        rot = -2 * t + 0.7;\n        const num11 = -(Math.PI * 2) / 5 * 0.5 + (1 - t) * ((Math.PI * 2) / 5) + Math.PI / 10;\n        // GetFrontHandPosition(Full, num11-π/2)（:49887-49913，dir=1 展开）\n        const hx = 10 * Math.cos(num11) - 4 - 3 * Math.sin(num11);\n        const hy = 10 * Math.sin(num11) - 2 + 3 * Math.cos(num11);\n        offX = hx + (8 + (2 - 8) * t);\n        offY = p.h * 0.5 + hy;\n        break;\n      }\n      case 13: {\n        // 短剑突刺（:50423-50446）：复合手臂持位 GetFrontHandPosition(stretch, aim-π/2)，\n        // stretch 按 1-pAnim 档 Quarter(6)/ThreeQuarters(8)/Full(10)；绘制旋转 = aim\n        const n24 = 1 - pAnim;\n        const mult = n24 <= 0.33 ? 6 : n24 <= 0.66 ? 8 : 10;\n        rot = swing.aim ?? 0;\n        offX = mult * Math.cos(rot) - 4 - 3 * Math.sin(rot);\n        offY = p.h * 0.5 + mult * Math.sin(rot) - 2 + 3 * Math.cos(rot);\n        break;\n      }\n      case 14: {\n        // 头侧持（:50604-50617，夜光 4952）：X=中心+6、Y=顶+32+头饰偏移；身体行不改\n        offX = 6;\n        offY = 32 + HEADGEAR_OFFSET_Y[2];\n        break;\n      }\n      case 15: {\n        // 头侧持（:50618-50628，占卜球 5644）：Y=顶+28+头饰偏移\n        offX = 6;\n        offY = 28 + HEADGEAR_OFFSET_Y[2];\n        break;\n      }\n      case 16: {\n        // 委托静持（:50629-50633，独角兽棒 856 = holdStyle 1 特例：X=中心+4、Y=顶+34）\n        offX = 4;\n        offY = 34;\n        break;\n      }\n      default: {\n        // 7/8/10/11/12 等未入表族（本批物品无）回退 useStyle 1 挥砍\n        rot = (pAnim - 0.5) * -3.5 - 0.3;\n        offX = fw * 0.5 - 10;\n        offY = 24;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n    if (hide) return;\n    ctx.save();\n    ctx.translate(p.cx + p.facing * offX, p.y + offY);\n    if (p.facing === -1) ctx.scale(-1, 1); // 镜像空间：旋转自动随镜像取反（与原版 -direction 因子等价）\n    ctx.rotate(rot);\n    if (ar) {\n      // 原版 origin = (W*0.5 − W*0.5*dir, H)（PlayerDrawLayers.cs:3260）：\n      // dir=1 → 底左角 / dir=−1 → 底右角钉在 itemLocation（握点）旋转——\n      // 镜像空间里统一画在 (0,−fh) 即底左角=握点，镜像自动翻成底右角\n      ctx.drawImage(ar.img, ar.sx, ar.sy, ar.sw, ar.sh, 0, -fh, ar.sw, ar.sh);\n    } else if (icon) {\n      ctx.drawImage(icon, 0, -14, 14, 14);\n    }\n    ctx.restore();\n  }"

#!/usr/bin/env node
/**
 * 一次性诊断脚本的统一 runner(防孤儿进程)。
 *
 * 背景:2026-08-10 发现 7 个 `vite-node tools/_diag-gen.mjs` 孤儿进程,
 * 每个占满 1 核空转 4 小时(脚本 import 链带起不可退出的活句柄,TERM 信号都无法处理)。
 * 约定:以后所有 _diag-*.mjs 一律经本 runner 运行,不再直接 npm exec vite-node。
 *
 * 用法:
 *   node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [传给脚本的参数...]
 *
 * 双层架构:
 *   - 子进程(--_child,内部):导入目标脚本;导入完成 0.5s 后强制 exit(0),
 *     不依赖事件循环自然排空——兜住"脚本主体完成但残留 interval/server 等活句柄"(本次事故形态);
 *   - 父进程(默认):只等子进程退出 + 看门狗;子进程超时(默认 120s)未退 → SIGKILL,exit 2。
 *     父进程事件循环始终空闲,因此子进程无论是同步死循环、永不 resolve 的 await
 *     还是任何未知卡死,都必然在 timeout 内被强杀。
 *
 * 退出码:0 正常 / 1 脚本异常或被信号终止 / 2 超时强杀 / 64 用法错误,其余透传脚本自设退出码。
 */

 */
const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000;
const FLUSH_MS = 500;

const argv = process.argv.slice(2);

// ---------- 子进程模式:导入目标脚本 ----------
if (argv[0] === '--_child') {
  const target = argv[1];
  try {
    const { pathToFileURL } = await import('node:url');
    const path = await import('node:path');
    await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(process.cwd(), target)).href);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[run-diag] 脚本异常:', e);
    process.exit(1);
  }
  // 主体完成:给 stdout 冲刷留 0.5s,无视残留活句柄强制正常退出。
  setTimeout(() => process.exit(0), FLUSH_MS).unref?.();
} else {
  // ---------- 父进程模式:spawn 子进程 + 看门狗 ----------
  let timeoutMs = DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;
  const rest = [];
  for (const a of argv) {
    if (a.startsWith('--timeout=')) {
      const v = Number(a.slice('--timeout='.length));
      if (Number.isFinite(v) && v > 0) timeoutMs = v;
    } else {
      rest.push(a);
    }
  }
  if (!rest.length) {
    console.error('usage: node tools/run-diag.mjs [--timeout=ms] <script.mjs> [args...]');
    process.exit(64);
  }

  const { spawn } = await import('node:child_process');
  const path = await import('node:path');
  const target = path.resolve(process.cwd(), rest[0]);

  const { fileURLToPath } = await import('node:url');
  const self = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
  const child = spawn(process.execPath, [self, '--_child', target, ...rest.slice(1)], {
    stdio: 'inherit',
  });

  let done = false;
  const watchdog = setTimeout(() => {
    if (done) return;
    done = true;
    console.error(`[run-diag] 超时 ${timeoutMs}ms,SIGKILL 子进程 (exit 2)`);

The convention's concern is orphan processes; using shell `timeout` + ensuring the script calls process.exit(0) explicitly mitigates. Better: put the probe at /tmp and run via a small wrapper that force-exits.

Actually cleanest: write the probe to import ONLY the data modules I need (vanillaItemFunc, vanillaItemCombat, items.ts, Inventory) — items.ts imports? It imports json + maybe sprites vanilla.json. Game.ts import is the heavy one (entities, net, etc.). For heldUseStyle I wanted Game.prototype — that imports all of Game's deps → heavy handles.

- For the Game dispatch, assert via source-level? weak.

Hmm. But actually heldUseStyle's logic is trivial and mirrors itemfunc; the probe asserting itemfunc per family sample IS the routing assertion (Game just forwards). Plus LEGACY_USE_STYLE map — I can assert by reading the Game.ts source? Meh.

Compromise: import Game with vite-node but explicitly process.exit(0) at the end, run under `timeout`. Game.ts module-level side effects? It imports many modules; module init might create handles (Audio? only on construct). vite-node transform of a 7800-line file is fine.
